| rel:api.oauthflows#3             | judgment_call (0.35)     | relationship_redundant (0.68)    | 1.1 | 1.7 | 0.26  | 0.09 | The associated-with relationships from api.oauthflows to api.components and api.operation are indirect, running through SecurityScheme, and duplicate those paths.                                                                                                                                       | Remove the associated-with relationships to api.components and api.operation.                                                                              |
| rel:api.example#3                | judgment_call (0.42)     | relationship_redundant (0.80)    | 1.1 | 1.7 | 0.14  | 0.06 | A path via parameter, header or media type may make both the api.example references-schema and associated-with-operation relationships redundant.                                                                                                                                                        | Drop both relationships if the indirect paths through parameter, header and media type already express the connection.                                     |
| rel:api.header#1                 | judgment_call (0.47)     | relationship_direction (0.57)    | 1.1 | 1.6 | 0.29  | 0.37 | The associated-with relationship from api.header to api.securityscheme is weak because the OpenAPI document has no structural link between them, only an apiKey scheme naming the same header string, so it should be derived or inferred rather than declared.                                          | Make the header-to-securityscheme association a derived or inferred relationship instead of a declared one.                                                |
| node:api.info#2                  | judgment_call (0.32)     | relationship_redundant (0.82)    | 1.1 | 1.6 | 0.18  | 0.07 | The outgoing 'associated-with' relationship from api.info to api.openapidocument duplicates the incoming 'composes' relationship between the same pair of types.                                                                                                                                         | Remove the info→openapidocument 'associated-with' relationship and keep the 'composes' edge.                                                               |
| rel:api.example#2                | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_redundant (0.35)    | 1.1 | 1.6 | 0.52  | 0.06 | The relationship api.example --[associated-with]--> api.operation is indirect, because an Example attaches to an operation only through a parameter, header or media type.                                                                                                                               | Remove the direct associated-with relationship to api.operation and rely on the path through parameter, header or media type.                              |
| node:api.response#3              | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_direction (0.83)    | 1.1 | 1.4 | 0.17  | 0.06 | The relationship 'api.mediatype --serves--> api.response' points in the reverse of the natural direction.                                                                                                                                                                                                | Reverse the relationship so it goes from api.response to api.mediatype, or replace it with a composition from response to mediatype.                       |
| rel:api.securityscheme#6         | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_direction (0.73)    | 1.1 | 1.4 | 0.04  | 0.03 | The satisfies edge from api.securityscheme to motivation.principle conflicts with the Principle definition, which says it is not directly verifiable.                                                                                                                                                    | Replace satisfies with governed-by-principles.                                                                                                             |
| node:api.oauthflow#3             | judgment_call (0.37)     | relationship_direction (0.58)    | 1.0 | 1.6 | 0.08  | 0.09 | The outgoing relationship from api.oauthflow to api.ratelimit looks spurious because an OAuth flow is consumed only via its SecurityScheme.                                                                                                                                                              | Remove the relationship from api.oauthflow to api.ratelimit.                                                                                               |
| rel:api.callback#2               | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_redundant (0.68)    | 1.0 | 1.8 | 0.09  | 0.05 | The api.callback uses api.server relationship is redundant because any servers override lives on the callback's PathItem or operation, so it duplicates a pathitem-to-server relationship and can be derived transitively through api.callback aggregates api.pathitem.                                  | Delete api.callback uses api.server and derive the server link transitively via the aggregated api.pathitem.                                               |
| rel:api.server#5                 | judgment_call (0.36)     | relationship_redundant (0.74)    | 1.0 | 1.7 | 0.18  | 0.08 | The relationship api.server serves api.pathitem overlaps with plain structural scoping already expressed by the pathitem aggregating servers.                                                                                                                                                            | Remove the serves relationship from api.server to api.pathitem.                                                                                            |
| rel:api.link#2                   | judgment_call (0.37)     | relationship_redundant (0.54)    | 1.0 | 1.7 | 0.16  | 0.06 | The relationship api.link references api.tag has no corresponding field in the OpenAPI Link Object and is only indirect via the target operation's tags; it is derivable from link→operation plus operation→tag.                                                                                         | Remove the api.link references api.tag relationship, since it is derivable from link→operation and operation→tag.                                          |
| rel:api.license#2                | judgment_call (0.56)     | relationship_direction (0.56)    | 1.0 | 1.3 | 0.28  | 0.03 | The api.info --aggregates--> api.license relationship understates the structural nesting of License inside Info; the containment should be modeled as composition (License nested in Info, composed into OpenAPIDocument) rather than aggregation or loose association.                                  | Change the Info-to-License relationship to composes, or express License containment via Info, instead of a direct association to the document.             |

| rel:api.server#1                 | judgment_call (0.71)     | relationship_direction (0.57)    | 1.0 | 1.5 | 0.11  | 0.09 | The relationship api.server aggregates api.servervariable should use composes, since server variables have no existence outside their server.                                                                                                                                                            | Replace aggregates with composes for server to servervariable.                                                                                             |
| rel:api.servervariable#1         | judgment_call (0.52)     | relationship_direction (0.83)    | 1.0 | 1.5 | 0.17  | 0.04 | The relationship api.servervariable --[governs]--> api.server understates the link, since a server variable is structurally part of its server and cannot exist without it, so 'composes' would fit better.                                                                                              | Replace the 'governs' predicate with 'composes' for the servervariable-to-server relationship.                                                             |
| node:api.paths#5                 | judgment_call (0.49)     | missing_attribute (0.52)         | 0.9 | 1.3 | 0.07  | 0.04 | api.paths has no way to represent x- specification extension keys allowed by OpenAPI on the Paths Object.                                                                                                                                                                                                | Consider adding support for x- extension keys on api.paths.                                                                                                |

| rel:api.contact#1                | judgment_call (0.60)     | relationship_redundant (0.84) ×2 | 0.9 | 1.5 | 0.15  | 0.04 | The relationship api.contact associated-with api.openapidocument is an indirect shortcut, because Contact is structurally nested in the Info object, which the document composes, and it may be redundant once a containment link via api.info exists.                                                   | Consider removing the document-level associated-with relationship from api.contact to api.openapidocument once containment via api.info is expressed.      |

| node:apm.resource#2              | judgment_call (0.38)     | attribute_typing (0.75)          | 1.2 | 1.7 | 0.06  | 0.07 | The attribute apm.resource.droppedAttributesCount is required but is rarely observable from code, so it should be optional with a default of 0.                                                                                                                                                               | Make droppedAttributesCount optional with a default value of 0.                                                                                                                       |
| node:apm.instrumentationscope#3  | judgment_call (0.33)     | relationship_direction (0.70) ×2 | 1.2 | 1.7 | 0.13  | 0.12 | The 'aggregates' relationship from apm.instrumentationscope to apm.exporterconfig is questionable because exporters are configured at provider level, not per scope.                                                                                                                                          | Remove or reconsider the aggregates relationship from instrumentationscope to exporterconfig.                                                                                         |
| node:apm.metricinstrument#2      | judgment_call (0.34)     | missing_attribute (0.68) ×3      | 1.2 | 1.0 | 0.14  | 0.05 | apm.metricinstrument has no attribute for recorded attribute keys (dimensions).                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Add an attribute listing the attribute keys (dimensions) recorded by the instrument.                                                                                                  |
| node:apm.alert#2                 | judgment_call (0.54)     | missing_attribute (0.69) ×4      | 1.2 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The apm.alert type lacks a labels attribute for arbitrary key-value metadata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Add a labels attribute to apm.alert.                                                                                                                                                  |
| rel:apm.logrecord#3              | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_redundant (0.83)    | 1.2 | 0.9 | 0.05  | 0.11 | The 'references' relationships from apm.logrecord to data-model.objectschema and data-model.schemadefinition overlap heavily in meaning.                                                                                                                                                                      | Consolidate the two relationships into one, or define a clear distinction between them.                                                                                               |
| rel:apm.resource#1               | judgment_call (0.38)     | relationship_direction (0.86)    | 1.2 | 1.6 | 0.06  | 0.06 | The relationships apm.resource monitors technology.node and technology.systemsoftware use the wrong predicate, since a Resource passively identifies its host or runtime rather than observing it.                                                                                                            | Replace monitors with references or associated-with for Node and SystemSoftware.                                                                                                      |
| rel:apm.dashboard#3              | judgment_call (0.44)     | relationship_direction (0.86) ×3 | 1.2 | 1.5 | 0.10  | 0.06 | The predicate monitors for apm.dashboard to apm.alert does not fit, because the dashboard displays alert state rather than observing a running system; references or associated-with is closer.                                                                                                               | Change the predicate from monitors to references or associated-with for the dashboard-to-alert relationship.                                                                          |
| node:apm.exporterconfig#2        | judgment_call (0.42)     | attribute_typing (0.57)          | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.13  | 0.04 | The apm.exporterconfig.protocol attribute is a free string but should be an enum with values grpc, http/protobuf and http/json.                                                                                                                                                                               | Change protocol to a string enum [grpc/http/protobuf/http/json].                                                                                                                      |
| rel:apm.instrumentationconfig#3  | judgment_call (0.41)     | relationship_direction (0.85)    | 1.1 | 1.4 | 0.16  | 0.25 | The relationship apm.instrumentationconfig serves apm.instrumentationscope uses a predicate that does not fit; 'uses' or 'generates' is closer than 'serves'.                                                                                                                                                 | Replace the serves predicate with 'uses' or 'generates' for the relationship to apm.instrumentationscope.                                                                             |

| node:apm.metricinstrument#3      | judgment_call (0.49)     | missing_attribute (0.64) ×3      | 1.1 | 1.0 | 0.06  | 0.05 | apm.metricinstrument has no attribute for histogram bucket boundaries (advisory parameters).                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Add an attribute for histogram explicit bucket boundaries (advisory parameters).                                                                                                      |

| rel:apm.exporterconfig#3         | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.1 | 1.8 | 0.26  | 0.06 | The relationship apm.exporterconfig satisfies security.retentionpolicy is weak because the backend, not the exporter, enforces retention.                                                                                                                                                                     | Remove the satisfies relationship to security.retentionpolicy.                                                                                                                        |
| node:apm.instrumentationconfig#2 | judgment_call (0.44)     | attribute_typing (0.48)          | 1.1 | 1.4 | 0.10  | 0.12 | The attribute 'type' on apm.instrumentationconfig is an unconstrained free string and should be limited to a fixed set of values.                                                                                                                                                                             | Constrain 'type' with an enum.                                                                                                                                                        |
| node:apm.alert#3                 | judgment_call (0.53)     | missing_attribute (0.73) ×4      | 1.1 | 1.0 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The apm.alert type lacks a runbookUrl attribute linking to remediation documentation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Add a runbookUrl attribute to apm.alert.                                                                                                                                              |
| rel:apm.dashboard#1              | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.1 | 1.4 | 0.17  | 0.05 | The relationship apm.dashboard --[monitors]--> api.operation is indirect: dashboards observe telemetry (spans, metrics) about the operation rather than the API contract itself.                                                                                                                              | Replace the monitors predicate to api.operation with a weaker predicate such as references or associated-with, or remove it in favor of the telemetry relationships.                  |
| rel:apm.dashboard#2              | judgment_call (0.56)     | relationship_redundant (0.72)    | 1.1 | 1.2 | 0.13  | 0.08 | The relationship apm.dashboard --[monitors]--> api.operation overlaps with the dashboard's monitors relationships to apm.span and apm.metricinstrument, which already capture the telemetry about the operation.                                                                                              | Drop or consolidate the api.operation relationship given the span and metricinstrument monitors relationships.                                                                        |
| rel:apm.resource#4               | judgment_call (0.50)     | relationship_redundant (0.43)    | 1.1 | 1.6 | 0.05  | 0.08 | The relationship apm.resource references security.secureresource is weak and overlaps with the maps-to relationship to application.applicationcomponent.                                                                                                                                                      | Remove the references relationship to SecureResource and keep maps-to ApplicationComponent.                                                                                           |
| rel:apm.alert#1                  | judgment_call (0.52)     | relationship_redundant (0.75)    | 1.1 | 1.7 | 0.11  | 0.12 | The relationships apm.alert monitors api.operation and apm.alert monitors api.ratelimit are indirect, since alerts evaluate telemetry (spans, metrics, logs) describing these API elements, so they may be redundant with span/metric monitoring plus span-to-operation traceability.                         | Consider removing apm.alert monitors api.operation and apm.alert monitors api.ratelimit in favor of alert-to-span/metric monitoring plus span-to-operation traceability.              |

| rel:apm.metricinstrument#4       | judgment_call (0.53)     | relationship_direction (0.85)    | 1.0 | 1.5 | 0.03  | 0.04 | The relationship apm.metricinstrument maps-to motivation.outcome uses a predicate that does not fit; it should be measures-outcome.                                                                                                                                                                           | Replace the maps-to predicate with measures-outcome for the relationship to motivation.outcome.                                                                                       |
| node:apm.alert#4                 | judgment_call (0.53)     | missing_attribute (0.64) ×4      | 1.0 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.05 | The apm.alert type lacks a 'tool' attribute naming the hosting monitoring tool, which apm.dashboard has.                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Add a tool attribute to apm.alert, consistent with apm.dashboard.                                                                                                                     |

## application

| id                                          | validity (p)             | category (p)                     | sev | fix | addr. | clf. | claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | proposed change                                                                                                                                                                   |

| node:application.applicationinteraction#3   | real_defect (0.46)       | enum_gap (0.85) ×2               | 1.9 | 1.0 | 0.10  | 0.05 | The 'pattern' enum of application.applicationinteraction lacks saga and orchestration style values.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Add saga and orchestration values to the pattern enum.                                                                                                                            |
| rel:application.applicationinteraction#2    | real_defect (0.64)       | relationship_direction (0.73)    | 1.8 | 1.7 | 0.05  | 0.04 | The depends-on relationship from application.applicationinteraction to application.applicationcollaboration inverts ArchiMate semantics, because the collaboration should be assigned to the interaction.                                                                                                               | Replace it with an assigned-to relationship from ApplicationCollaboration to ApplicationInteraction.                                                                              |
| node:application.applicationevent#5         | real_defect (0.38)       | relationship_direction (0.81)    | 1.7 | 1.5 | 0.06  | 0.04 | The relationship data-store.eventhandler triggers application.applicationevent appears to have an inverted direction.                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Reverse the direction so the applicationevent triggers the eventhandler, or otherwise correct the predicate.                                                                      |
| rel:application.applicationevent#1          | real_defect (0.69)       | relationship_direction (0.73)    | 1.6 | 1.5 | 0.04  | 0.05 | The relationship application.applicationevent triggers application.applicationcollaboration is semantically wrong because in ArchiMate triggers links behaviour elements while a collaboration is active structure.                                                                                                     | Replace the triggers relationship to ApplicationCollaboration with a triggers relationship from ApplicationEvent to ApplicationInteraction.                                       |
| node:application.applicationservice#2       | real_defect (0.67)       | enum_gap (0.87)                  | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.02  | 0.03 | The enum values of application.applicationservice.serviceType overlap because it mixes timing (synchronous/asynchronous) with style (event-driven/batch) on one axis.                                                                                                                                                   | Separate timing and style into distinct axes or attributes, or redefine the enum values so they do not overlap.                                                                   |
| node:application.applicationfunction#3      | real_defect (0.39)       | relationship_direction (0.81)    | 1.6 | 1.7 | 0.12  | 0.07 | The relationship api.operation realizes application.applicationfunction appears inverted; operations normally realize or serve services rather than functions.                                                                                                                                                          | Reverse or replace the relationship so operations realize or serve application services (or the function realizes the operation) instead of the operation realizing the function. |
| node:application.applicationinterface#1     | real_defect (0.59)       | enum_gap (0.77) ×3               | 1.5 | 1.7 | 0.02  | 0.04 | The protocol enum of application.applicationinterface mixes true protocols with architectural styles (REST, GraphQL).                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Separate protocols from architectural styles, e.g. remove or relocate REST and GraphQL so the enum holds a single axis.                                                           |
| node:application.applicationinterface#4     | real_defect (0.36)       | relationship_redundant (0.81) ×3 | 1.5 | 1.7 | 0.07  | 0.09 | application.applicationinterface has both depends-on and serves relationships to application.applicationservice, which express the same meaning and are redundant.                                                                                                                                                      | Remove one of the two relationships, keeping the one with the correct semantics.                                                                                                  |
| node:application.applicationcomponent#2     | real_defect (0.57)       | enum_gap (0.80)                  | 1.5 | 1.8 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The 'type' enum on application.applicationcomponent mixes two axes, ownership (internal/external) and style (service-component), and includes 'generic' as a catch-all.                                                                                                                                                 | Split 'type' into two attributes, 'sourcing' (internal/external) and 'kind' (e.g. service-component, generic).                                                                    |
| rel:application.applicationprocess#2        | real_defect (0.55)       | relationship_redundant (0.47)    | 1.5 | 1.5 | 0.05  | 0.09 | The depends-on and triggers relationships between application.applicationprocess and application.applicationevent have unclear direction, since it is ambiguous whether they mean an inbound trigger or an emission.                                                                                                    | Clarify the semantics so that inbound triggering is distinguished from event emission, and remove the overlapping depends-on.                                                     |
| rel:application.applicationinterface#2      | real_defect (0.38)       | relationship_direction (0.58)    | 1.4 | 1.6 | 0.06  | 0.04 | ApplicationInterface depends-on ApplicationComponent inverts ArchiMate composition, in which the component is composed of the interface.                                                                                                                                                                                | Reverse it to a component-to-interface composition (component composed-of interface) or drop the depends-on.                                                                      |
| rel:application.applicationcollaboration#4  | real_defect (0.36)       | relationship_direction (0.50)    | 1.4 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The provides relationship from application.applicationcollaboration to application.applicationinterface conflicts with the interface definition, which says an interface is owned by a component.                                                                                                                       | none stated                                                                                                                                                                       |
| node:application.applicationinterface#2     | real_defect (0.63)       | enum_gap (0.56) ×3               | 1.4 | 1.7 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The protocol enum of application.applicationinterface has HTTP and HTTPS as separate values, splitting on TLS, which contradicts the attribute's own guidance that transport-level concerns like TLS belong in the Technology Layer.                                                                                    | Merge HTTP and HTTPS into a single HTTP value.                                                                                                                                    |
| rel:application.applicationinterface#1      | real_defect (0.40)       | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.4 | 1.7 | 0.02  | 0.04 | The relationship ApplicationInterface delivers-value to ApplicationCollaboration is odd because delivers-value is a traceability value predicate targeting an active-structure grouping; aggregation or assignment fits better.                                                                                         | Replace delivers-value from ApplicationInterface to ApplicationCollaboration with an aggregation or assignment relationship.                                                      |
| rel:application.applicationinteraction#1    | real_defect (0.44)       | relationship_redundant (0.45) ×2 | 1.3 | 1.7 | 0.03  | 0.04 | The delivers-value relationship from application.applicationinteraction to application.applicationservice is redundant with the realizes relationship between the same pair, and delivers-value is a traceability predicate meant for business value/goals, so it is misused here.                                      | Remove the delivers-value relationship from ApplicationInteraction to ApplicationService and keep realizes.                                                                       |
| rel:application.applicationservice#1        | real_defect (0.36)       | relationship_redundant (0.90)    | 1.3 | 1.5 | 0.02  | 0.04 | The relationships application.applicationservice accesses application.dataobject and application.applicationservice depends-on application.dataobject overlap in meaning.                                                                                                                                               | Remove or clearly differentiate one of the accesses/depends-on relationships to DataObject.                                                                                       |
| rel:application.applicationfunction#2       | real_defect (0.44)       | relationship_redundant (0.89)    | 1.2 | 1.8 | 0.03  | 0.05 | The depends-on relationship from application.applicationfunction to application.dataobject overlaps with the accesses relationship between the same two types.                                                                                                                                                          | Remove the depends-on relationship to DataObject and keep accesses.                                                                                                               |
| node:application.dataobject#6               | real_defect (0.44)       | relationship_direction (0.51)    | 1.2 | 1.3 | 0.04  | 0.05 | The self-referencing 'realizes' relationship on application.dataobject has unclear semantics.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Clarify the semantics of the self-referencing realizes relationship or remove it.                                                                                                 |
| rel:application.dataobject#1                | real_defect (0.64)       | relationship_direction (0.69)    | 1.2 | 0.5 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The direction of the 'composes' relationship from application.dataobject to application.dataobject is ambiguous: its inverse 'composed-of' implies the source is the part, whereas ArchiMate and the parallel 'aggregates' relationship make the source the whole.                                                      | Clarify and document that the source of 'composes' is the whole (aligned with ArchiMate and 'aggregates'), and make the inverse name 'composed-of' consistent with that.          |

| node:application.applicationinterface#3     | judgment_call (0.45)     | enum_gap (0.84) ×3               | 1.9 | 1.1 | 0.02  | 0.03 | The protocol enum of application.applicationinterface lacks values for CLI, Kafka, MQTT and in-process/library APIs.                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Add CLI, Kafka, MQTT and in-process/library API values to the protocol enum.                                                                                                      |

| rel:application.applicationcollaboration#1  | judgment_call (0.36)     | relationship_direction (0.79)    | 1.5 | 1.8 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The delivers-value relationships from application.applicationcollaboration to application.applicationinteraction and application.applicationservice use a traceability predicate, whereas ArchiMate would use assigned-to for collaboration to interaction and realizes for collaboration to service.                   | Replace delivers-value with assigned-to for collaboration to interaction and with realizes for collaboration to service.                                                          |
| rel:application.applicationcomponent#1      | judgment_call (0.35)     | relationship_redundant (0.87)    | 1.4 | 1.9 | 0.04  | 0.05 | The relationships assigned-to and composes from application.applicationcomponent to application.applicationfunction overlap in meaning.                                                                                                                                                                                 | Keep only assigned-to for component-to-function and remove the redundant composes relationship.                                                                                   |

| rel:application.applicationprocess#1        | judgment_call (0.46)     | relationship_direction (0.65)    | 1.3 | 1.6 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The delivers-value relationship from application.applicationprocess to application.applicationservice uses a business-value traceability predicate, whereas process-to-service is classically ArchiMate realizes.                                                                                                       | Replace delivers-value with realizes for ApplicationProcess to ApplicationService.                                                                                                |

| node:business.product#1               | real_defect (0.59)       | definition_conflict (0.59)       | 1.3 | 1.2 | 0.87  | 0.20 | The definition of business.product calls Product a passive structure element, but in ArchiMate 3.2 it is a composite element.                                                                                                                                                  | Redefine business.product as a composite element that bundles services and a contract offered as a whole.                                                                      |

| rel:business.businessfunction#2       | real_defect (0.46)       | relationship_direction (0.68)    | 1.0 | 0.2 | 0.05  | 0.03 | The composes self-relationship on business.businessfunction has ambiguous direction (whole vs. part as source) and is not documented explicitly.                                                                                                                               | Document explicitly which end (whole or part) is the source of the composes relationship.                                                                                      |
| node:business.businessevent#4         | real_defect (0.51)       | definition_conflict (0.72)       | 0.9 | 0.2 | 0.93  | 0.04 | The business.businessevent definition should mention BusinessInteraction as something it can trigger or result from, to match the relationships.                                                                                                                               | Add BusinessInteraction to the definition alongside BusinessProcess and BusinessFunction.                                                                                      |
| node:business.businessinteraction#1   | real_defect (0.56)       | definition_conflict (0.69) ×3    | 0.8 | 0.1 | 0.71  | 0.18 | The definition of business.businessinteraction wrongly states that BusinessProcess is performed by a role and BusinessFunction by an actor, whereas ArchiMate allows either to be assigned to actors or roles.                                                                 | Reword the definition to drop the role-only/actor-only contrast between BusinessProcess and BusinessFunction.                                                                  |

| node:business.businessinteraction#3   | judgment_call (0.41)     | relationship_missing (0.79)      | 1.6 | 1.0 | 0.10  | 0.03 | A 'realizes' relationship from business.businessinteraction to business.businessservice is missing from the catalog.                                                                                                                                                           | Add a realizes relationship from businessinteraction to businessservice.                                                                                                       |

| node:business.businessprocess#3       | judgment_call (0.35)     | relationship_redundant (0.82) ×2 | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.11  | 0.04 | The businessrole relationships 'assigned-to' and 'performs' toward businessprocess are duplicates expressing the same meaning.                                                                                                                                                 | Keep one of the two relationships and remove the other.                                                                                                                        |
| node:business.businessservice#3       | judgment_call (0.42)     | relationship_direction (0.65)    | 1.4 | 1.5 | 0.05  | 0.04 | The 'business.businessservice delivers business.businessobject' relationship is non-standard; ArchiMate would use access.                                                                                                                                                      | Replace 'delivers' with an 'accesses' relationship to businessobject.                                                                                                          |
| node:business.product#2               | judgment_call (0.50)     | relationship_missing (0.70)      | 1.4 | 1.1 | 0.45  | 0.05 | business.product relationships cover only business services and contracts, but ArchiMate also allows products to aggregate application services, technology services, business objects and data objects.                                                                       | Add aggregation relationships from business.product to application services, technology services, business objects and data objects.                                           |

| node:business.businessobject#1        | judgment_call (0.55)     | relationship_missing (0.42) ×2   | 1.3 | 1.4 | 0.16  | 0.29 | In ArchiMate, Contract is a specialization of BusinessObject, but in this spec business.contract and business.businessobject are modelled as siblings with no specialization relationship.                                                                                     | Model contract as a specialization of businessobject or add an explicit specialization relationship between them.                                                              |
| node:business.businessactor#1         | judgment_call (0.35)     | relationship_direction (0.48)    | 1.3 | 1.8 | 0.17  | 0.06 | The relationship businessrole --serves--> businessactor departs from ArchiMate, where a role is reached from an actor only by assignment.                                                                                                                                      | Remove the serves relationship from businessrole to businessactor and rely on assignment from actor to role.                                                                   |
| rel:business.businessfunction#1       | judgment_call (0.51)     | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.3 | 1.6 | 0.04  | 0.03 | The relationship business.businessfunction realizes motivation.goal is questionable because ArchiMate lets core elements realize requirements and influence goals, so 'supports' or 'influence' fits better.                                                                   | Replace the realizes predicate to motivation.goal with 'supports' or 'influences'.                                                                                             |
| rel:business.businesscollaboration#1  | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_direction (0.58) ×2 | 1.2 | 1.4 | 0.26  | 0.04 | The composes relationship from business.businesscollaboration to business.businessrole conflicts with ArchiMate semantics because roles exist independently of any collaboration, so it should be aggregation rather than composition.                                         | Replace the composes relationship with an aggregates relationship from BusinessCollaboration to BusinessRole.                                                                  |

| rel:business.businessinterface#3      | judgment_call (0.47)     | relationship_direction (0.70)    | 1.2 | 1.4 | 0.13  | 0.05 | The triggers relationship from business.businessinterface to business.businessfunction is especially doubtful because a BusinessFunction has no defined start.                                                                                                                 | Replace it with serves, or with assigned-to via the service.                                                                                                                   |

## data-store

| id                                | validity (p)             | category (p)                     | sev | fix | addr. | clf. | claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | proposed change                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------------------- | --- | --- | ----- | ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| node:data-store.accesspattern#3   | real_defect (0.37)       | attribute_typing (0.37)          | 1.8 | 1.7 | 0.15  | 0.04 | A single-valued targetCollection on data-store.accesspattern cannot express join or multi-collection transaction access patterns.                                                                                                                                  | Allow multiple target collections, for example by using the relationship to collection with multiplicity instead of a single string.                                                |
| node:data-store.namespace#2       | real_defect (0.40)       | relationship_direction (0.67) ×2 | 1.7 | 1.7 | 0.39  | 0.17 | The relationship 'collection composes namespace' appears inverted, since a namespace should contain collections, or it skips a level.                                                                                                                              | Reverse the relationship so namespace composes collection, or remove it.                                                                                                            |
| node:data-store.collection#4      | real_defect (0.51)       | relationship_direction (0.80) ×2 | 1.7 | 1.6 | 0.38  | 0.14 | The outgoing relationship 'collection composes namespace' appears inverted, since a namespace should compose collections.                                                                                                                                          | Reverse the direction so namespace composes collection.                                                                                                                             |
| rel:data-store.collection#1       | real_defect (0.73)       | relationship_redundant (0.55)    | 1.7 | 1.8 | 0.14  | 0.08 | The relationship data-store.collection composes data-store.namespace is inverted, because namespaces contain collections (a reverse namespace-composes-collection already exists).                                                                                 | Remove collection-composes-namespace and keep only namespace-composes-collection.                                                                                                   |
| node:data-store.index#3           | real_defect (0.53)       | type_overlap (0.70)              | 1.6 | 0.6 | 0.94  | 0.86 | The Elasticsearch 'index' concept is claimed by both the collection and namespace definitions, creating ambiguity over which type models it, in relation to data-store.index.                                                                                      | Define a boundary/precedence rule assigning the Elasticsearch index to a single type (collection).                                                                                  |
| node:data-store.eventhandler#1    | real_defect (0.42)       | enum_gap (0.74)                  | 1.6 | 1.0 | 0.17  | 0.07 | The handlerType values WATCHER and ALERT_RULE fire on periodic conditions rather than data changes, but the timing enum on data-store.eventhandler has no SCHEDULED/PERIODIC value to express this.                                                                | Add a SCHEDULED or PERIODIC value to the timing enum.                                                                                                                               |
| node:data-store.index#2           | real_defect (0.39)       | attribute_typing (0.53)          | 1.6 | 1.9 | 0.09  | 0.04 | The data-store.index fields attribute is a plain string, so it cannot express per-field sort direction or operator class.                                                                                                                                          | Change fields to a structured list of field entries each with name, sort direction, and opclass.                                                                                    |
| node:data-store.namespace#3       | real_defect (0.55)       | relationship_direction (0.52)    | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.38  | 0.11 | The relationship 'namespace composes field' appears to skip a level, because fields belong to collections rather than directly to namespaces.                                                                                                                      | Remove the direct namespace-to-field composition and route it through collection.                                                                                                   |
| node:data-store.index#1           | real_defect (0.54)       | enum_gap (0.74)                  | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.28  | 0.06 | The data-store.index method enum mixes access methods (e.g. BTREE) with key shapes (COMPOUND, MULTIKEY), which are orthogonal axes.                                                                                                                                | Remove COMPOUND and MULTIKEY from the method enum and represent key shape separately (e.g. derived from fields or a separate attribute).                                            |
| rel:data-store.accesspattern#1    | real_defect (0.68)       | relationship_direction (0.49)    | 1.6 | 1.7 | 0.25  | 0.07 | The relationship data-store.accesspattern aggregates data-store.collection duplicates accesses and misuses whole-part semantics, since an access pattern targets rather than contains a collection.                                                                | Remove the aggregates relationship from accesspattern to collection and keep accesses.                                                                                              |
| rel:data-store.field#1            | real_defect (0.44)       | relationship_direction (0.87)    | 1.5 | 1.7 | 0.07  | 0.14 | The relationship data-store.field triggers data-store.validationrule uses a predicate that does not fit, because declarative constraints restrict field values rather than being initiated by them; constrained-by would model it better.                          | Replace the 'triggers' predicate with 'constrained-by' for the field to validationrule relationship.                                                                                |
| node:data-store.storedlogic#1     | real_defect (0.40)       | definition_conflict (0.48)       | 1.5 | 1.7 | 0.76  | 0.70 | The logicType value LAMBDA contradicts the definition of storedlogic as logic that executes within the data store engine, since DynamoDB Lambda triggers run outside the engine and are better modelled as an eventhandler bound to external application code.     | Remove LAMBDA from logicType and model DynamoDB Lambda triggers as an eventhandler bound to external application code.                                                              |
| rel:data-store.retentionpolicy#2  | real_defect (0.38)       | relationship_direction (0.53)    | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.26  | 0.07 | A retention policy does not contain containers as parts but scopes and rules them, so governs is the correct predicate rather than aggregates toward Collection and Namespace.                                                                                     | Remove aggregates in favor of governs.                                                                                                                                              |
| node:data-store.view#1            | real_defect (0.65)       | definition_conflict (0.39)       | 1.4 | 1.7 | 0.18  | 0.05 | The data-store.view attribute 'materialized' is redundant with 'viewType' and can contradict it (e.g. viewType ALIAS with materialized=true).                                                                                                                      | Derive 'materialized' from viewType (or remove it) so the two cannot contradict each other.                                                                                         |
| node:data-store.retentionpolicy#5 | real_defect (0.41)       | missing_attribute (0.80)         | 1.4 | 1.0 | 0.09  | 0.04 | data-store.retentionpolicy lacks an attribute stating the age basis, i.e. whether age is measured from insert time or from a timestamp field.                                                                                                                      | Add an age-basis attribute that specifies insert time or a named timestamp field.                                                                                                   |
| rel:data-store.view#1             | real_defect (0.47)       | relationship_redundant (0.81)    | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.17  | 0.08 | The relationship data-store.view aggregates data-store.collection largely duplicates data-store.view derives-from data-store.collection, and derives-from is the more accurate semantics.                                                                          | Remove the aggregates relationship from data-store.view to data-store.collection and keep derives-from.                                                                             |
| rel:data-store.validationrule#2   | real_defect (0.45)       | relationship_redundant (0.75)    | 1.3 | 1.6 | 0.08  | 0.07 | The relationship data-store.validationrule satisfies motivation.requirement overlaps with the governance predicate enforces-requirement, so constraint-to-requirement traceability has two competing predicates.                                                   | Pick a single predicate (either satisfies or enforces-requirement) for constraint-to-requirement traceability across the spec.                                                      |
| node:data-store.storedlogic#2     | real_defect (0.57)       | definition_conflict (0.74)       | 1.3 | 1.3 | 0.61  | 0.15 | The language value EXTERNAL (logic implemented outside the database) contradicts the definition of storedlogic as logic that executes within the data store engine.                                                                                                | Remove EXTERNAL from the language enum or otherwise reconcile it with the in-engine execution definition.                                                                           |
| node:data-store.namespace#1       | real_defect (0.54)       | type_overlap (0.51)              | 1.3 | 0.7 | 0.89  | 0.85 | Mapping an Elasticsearch index to data-store.namespace conflicts with data-store.collection's SEARCH_INDEX type, so only one of the two should own Elasticsearch indexes.                                                                                          | Remove Elasticsearch index from the Namespace definition and map it to Collection (SEARCH_INDEX) only.                                                                              |
| node:data-store.index#5           | real_defect (0.40)       | relationship_redundant (0.89) ×2 | 1.3 | 1.9 | 0.04  | 0.06 | The relationships from AccessPattern to Index include redundant 'aggregates' and 'uses' relationships expressing the same meaning.                                                                                                                                 | Collapse the two relationships into one (keep uses) and remove the redundant aggregates.                                                                                            |
| node:data-store.storedlogic#4     | real_defect (0.43)       | enum_gap (0.53)                  | 1.3 | 1.7 | 0.07  | 0.09 | The logicType values PROCEDURE and STORED_PROCEDURE duplicate each other.                                                                                                                                                                                          | Merge PROCEDURE and STORED_PROCEDURE into a single value.                                                                                                                           |
| rel:data-store.accesspattern#2    | real_defect (0.45)       | relationship_redundant (0.59) ×2 | 1.3 | 1.6 | 0.13  | 0.08 | The relationship data-store.accesspattern aggregates data-store.index overlaps with the uses relationship to index, and uses is the correct predicate.                                                                                                             | Remove the aggregates relationship from accesspattern to index and keep uses.                                                                                                       |

| node:motivation.stakeholder#1 | real_defect (0.73)       | enum_gap (0.82) ×2               | 1.8 | 1.9 | 0.09  | 0.06 | The enum attribute 'type' on motivation.stakeholder mixes orthogonal axes (internal/external relationship, role such as sponsor/customer/end-user, and kind such as system/regulatory), so its values are not mutually exclusive.                                                              | Split the enum into separate attributes per axis (e.g. relationship to organization, role, kind) or otherwise make the values mutually exclusive.                                                       |
| rel:motivation.goal#2         | real_defect (0.42)       | relationship_redundant (0.81)    | 1.8 | 1.9 | 0.07  | 0.05 | The relationship motivation.goal realizes motivation.goal overlaps with motivation.goal aggregates motivation.goal and motivation.goal influence motivation.goal as variants of goal decomposition.                                                                                            | Consolidate the goal-to-goal decomposition relationships into one, with clear semantics for the rest.                                                                                                   |
| rel:motivation.goal#1         | real_defect (0.60)       | relationship_direction (0.60)    | 1.8 | 1.8 | 0.06  | 0.04 | The relationship motivation.goal aggregates motivation.requirement inverts the usual ArchiMate direction, in which requirements realize goals.                                                                                                                                                 | Remove or reverse the goal-aggregates-requirement relationship so that requirement realizes goal is the canonical form.                                                                                 |
| node:motivation.meaning#2     | real_defect (0.53)       | missing_attribute (0.55) ×2      | 1.7 | 1.1 | 0.36  | 0.06 | motivation.meaning has no attribute or reference identifying the concept or element being interpreted.                                                                                                                                                                                         | Add a reference attribute to the interpreted element on motivation.meaning.                                                                                                                             |
| rel:motivation.requirement#1  | real_defect (0.66)       | relationship_direction (0.52) ×2 | 1.7 | 1.8 | 0.08  | 0.05 | The relationship motivation.requirement --[aggregates]--> motivation.goal inverts the usual hierarchy, since goals are refined by requirements, so it should be removed or reversed.                                                                                                           | Remove the requirement aggregates goal relationship or reverse its direction.                                                                                                                           |
| node:motivation.principle#3   | real_defect (0.50)       | relationship_direction (0.55) ×2 | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.14  | 0.15 | Code-level types such as systemsoftware (implements) and applicationcomponent (realizes) relate directly to motivation.principle, bypassing requirements, whereas ArchiMate expects principles to be realized via requirements.                                                                | Remove the direct realization/implements relationships from code-level types to principle and route them through requirement.                                                                           |
| rel:motivation.principle#2    | real_defect (0.49)       | relationship_direction (0.60)    | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.07  | 0.03 | ArchiMate does not allow a principle to realize a goal, so the relationship motivation.principle realizes motivation.goal conflicts with the standard.                                                                                                                                         | Remove the realizes relationship from principle to goal, or restrict it to cases the standard permits.                                                                                                  |
| rel:motivation.constraint#1   | real_defect (0.53)       | relationship_redundant (0.87)    | 1.6 | 1.4 | 0.09  | 0.06 | The relationships motivation.constraint --[constrains]--> motivation.requirement and motivation.constraint --[influence]--> motivation.requirement both express a constraint shaping a requirement, so modelers will struggle to choose between them.                                          | Keep 'constrains' for limiting the solution space and reserve 'influence' for weaker, non-restrictive effects, or drop the influence relationship from motivation.constraint to motivation.requirement. |
| node:motivation.requirement#5 | real_defect (0.30)       | relationship_redundant (0.57)    | 1.5 | 1.6 | 0.06  | 0.04 | Incoming relationships to motivation.requirement use 'satisfies', 'realizes', and 'fulfills' inconsistently for similar meanings.                                                                                                                                                              | Standardize the incoming predicates to a single consistent predicate (e.g. realizes).                                                                                                                   |
| node:motivation.outcome#3     | real_defect (0.56)       | relationship_direction (0.74)    | 1.5 | 1.4 | 0.13  | 0.04 | The outcome 'realizes' value relationship is non-standard, since ArchiMate associates value with outcome rather than having outcome realize value.                                                                                                                                             | Replace the outcome 'realizes' value relationship with an association (or the ArchiMate-conformant relationship) between value and outcome.                                                             |
| node:motivation.value#1       | real_defect (0.34)       | relationship_direction (0.67)    | 1.5 | 1.7 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The relationships 'motivation.goal realizes motivation.value' and 'motivation.outcome realizes motivation.value' deviate from ArchiMate, which only associates value with other elements.                                                                                                      | Replace the realizes relationships from goal and outcome to value with associated-with, or otherwise align them with ArchiMate.                                                                         |
| node:motivation.assessment#1  | real_defect (0.45)       | enum_gap (0.79)                  | 1.5 | 1.8 | 0.09  | 0.08 | The assessmentType enum on motivation.assessment mixes analysis methods (SWOT) with subject kinds (risk, gap, impact), so it combines different axes.                                                                                                                                          | Split assessmentType into separate attributes or otherwise separate method values from subject-kind values.                                                                                             |
| node:motivation.principle#4   | real_defect (0.40)       | relationship_direction (0.59) ×2 | 1.4 | 1.4 | 0.09  | 0.03 | The relationship name 'governed-by-principles' is not a standard ArchiMate relationship name.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Rename or replace 'governed-by-principles' with a standard relationship predicate.                                                                                                                      |
| rel:motivation.goal#5         | real_defect (0.59)       | relationship_direction (0.67)    | 1.4 | 1.7 | 0.06  | 0.03 | The relationship motivation.goal influence motivation.assessment reverses the normal direction, in which an assessment influences a goal.                                                                                                                                                      | Remove goal influence assessment and rely on assessment influence goal.                                                                                                                                 |
| node:motivation.assessment#4  | real_defect (0.59)       | relationship_redundant (0.65)    | 1.0 | 1.2 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The relationship assessment-influence-assessment appears twice in the relationship list of motivation.assessment, which may be an unintended duplicate.                                                                                                                                        | Confirm whether the duplicate is intentional and remove it if it is not.                                                                                                                                |
| node:motivation.value#2       | real_defect (0.65)       | relationship_redundant (0.77) ×2 | 1.0 | 1.3 | 0.14  | 0.03 | The relationship 'motivation.value associated-with motivation.value' is listed twice in the catalog.                                                                                                                                                                                           | Remove the duplicate value associated-with value relationship so it is listed once.                                                                                                                     |
| node:motivation.constraint#1  | real_defect (0.41)       | definition_conflict (0.65)       | 1.0 | 0.1 | 0.88  | 0.11 | The definition of motivation.constraint says constraints are 'as opposed to requirements which must be satisfied', which is misleading because in ArchiMate a constraint is also binding, being a requirement-like element that restricts realization.                                         | Reword the definition to drop the contrast with requirements and state that a constraint is a binding restriction on how the system may be realized.                                                    |
| node:motivation.constraint#5  | real_defect (0.54)       | relationship_redundant (0.82)    | 0.8 | 1.2 | 0.06  | 0.02 | The self-loop 'specializes' relationship on motivation.constraint is listed twice, making it redundant.                                                                                                                                                                                        | Remove the duplicate specializes self-loop relationship so it is listed once.                                                                                                                           |
| node:motivation.constraint#3  | judgment_call (0.48)     | enum_gap (0.81) ×2               | 1.7 | 1.0 | 0.19  | 0.03 | The constraintType enum of motivation.constraint lacks 'contractual/legal' and 'security' values.                                                                                                                                                                                              | Add 'contractual' (or 'legal') and 'security' values to the constraintType enum.                                                                                                                        |
| node:motivation.principle#2   | judgment_call (0.54)     | enum_gap (0.88)                  | 1.6 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.03 | The category enum of motivation.principle lacks values for business and usability/UX domains.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Add business and usability (UX) values to the category enum.                                                                                                                                            |
| node:motivation.value#3       | judgment_call (0.48)     | enum_gap (0.85)                  | 1.5 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.03 | The motivation.value valueType enum lacks a customer or user-experience category, so modellers will overload 'social' or 'operational'.                                                                                                                                                        | Add a customer or user-experience value to the valueType enum.                                                                                                                                          |
| node:motivation.outcome#1     | judgment_call (0.52)     | missing_attribute (0.63)         | 1.4 | 1.2 | 0.26  | 0.05 | motivation.outcome has no attributes for measure, targetValue or targetDate, so measurability is only expressible in free text.                                                                                                                                                                | Add measure, targetValue and targetDate attributes to motivation.outcome.                                                                                                                               |
| rel:motivation.outcome#1      | judgment_call (0.42)     | relationship_redundant (0.81)    | 1.4 | 0.8 | 0.10  | 0.07 | The relationships motivation.outcome --influence--> motivation.goal and motivation.outcome --realizes--> motivation.goal overlap in meaning; modelers should use realizes for full attainment and influence for partial contribution.                                                          | Document a usage rule: realizes for full attainment of the goal, influence for partial contribution (or merge the two).                                                                                 |
| rel:motivation.assessment#1   | judgment_call (0.43)     | relationship_direction (0.46)    | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.07  | 0.05 | The associated-with relationships from motivation.assessment to motivation.constraint and motivation.outcome are inconsistent with the influence relationships from assessment to goal, requirement and principle; an assessment shaping an outcome or constraint would likewise be influence. | Replace the associated-with relationships to constraint and outcome with influence relationships.                                                                                                       |
| node:motivation.driver#1      | judgment_call (0.42)     | enum_gap (0.80) ×2               | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.06  | 0.05 | The motivation.driver 'category' enum mixes overlapping values: 'business' overlaps with market, strategic and competitive.                                                                                                                                                                    | Consolidate the category enum to remove 'business' overlap with market/strategic/competitive values.                                                                                                    |
| rel:motivation.principle#1    | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_redundant (0.82)    | 1.4 | 1.8 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The relationship motivation.principle realizes motivation.goal duplicates the meaning of motivation.principle influence motivation.goal, so two predicates express the same contribution between principle and goal.                                                                           | Remove the realizes relationship from principle to goal and keep influence.                                                                                                                             |
| rel:motivation.requirement#2  | judgment_call (0.44)     | relationship_redundant (0.83)    | 1.4 | 1.3 | 0.13  | 0.06 | The relationship motivation.requirement --[associated-with]--> motivation.goal overlaps with motivation.requirement --[realizes]--> motivation.goal, and realizes should be preferred when the requirement actually refines the goal.                                                          | Prefer realizes over associated-with for requirement-to-goal links where the requirement refines the goal, removing or restricting the associated-with relationship.                                    |
| node:motivation.requirement#6 | judgment_call (0.45)     | missing_attribute (0.65)         | 1.3 | 1.0 | 0.43  | 0.04 | motivation.requirement lacks an attribute for verification or acceptance criteria.                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Add a verification/acceptanceCriteria attribute to motivation.requirement.                                                                                                                              |
| node:motivation.assessment#3  | judgment_call (0.59)     | missing_attribute (0.70) ×2      | 1.3 | 1.1 | 0.06  | 0.06 | motivation.assessment lacks severity and likelihood attributes needed to describe risk findings.                                                                                                                                                                                               | Add severity and likelihood attributes to motivation.assessment for risk findings.                                                                                                                      |
| rel:motivation.goal#3         | judgment_call (0.46)     | relationship_redundant (0.85)    | 1.3 | 1.8 | 0.04  | 0.06 | The relationships motivation.goal influence motivation.principle and motivation.goal supports motivation.principle are largely redundant with each other.                                                                                                                                      | Keep only one of influence or supports between goal and principle.                                                                                                                                      |
| rel:motivation.value#1        | judgment_call (0.42)     | relationship_direction (0.68)    | 1.3 | 1.5 | 0.07  | 0.06 | Apart from value-to-stakeholder, the relationships from motivation.value use generic associated-with, which hides their meaning.                                                                                                                                                               | Replace generic associated-with with more specific predicates where a meaningful one exists.                                                                                                            |
| rel:motivation.outcome#3      | judgment_call (0.39)     | relationship_redundant (0.44)    | 1.3 | 1.7 | 0.07  | 0.03 | The associated-with relationship between motivation.outcome and motivation.driver is weak; a driver-to-outcome influence relationship would be more precise.                                                                                                                                   | Replace the outcome-to-driver associated-with with a driver --influence--> outcome relationship.                                                                                                        |
| node:motivation.constraint#4  | judgment_call (0.55)     | missing_attribute (0.71)         | 1.3 | 1.0 | 0.11  | 0.03 | motivation.constraint has no source or rationale attribute to record where the constraint comes from or why it exists.                                                                                                                                                                         | Add a source/rationale attribute to motivation.constraint.                                                                                                                                              |
| rel:motivation.assessment#2   | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_direction (0.80)    | 1.3 | 1.5 | 0.05  | 0.04 | The influence relationship from motivation.assessment to motivation.driver inverts ArchiMate's convention, where assessments are associated with the drivers they evaluate.                                                                                                                    | Replace the assessment-influence-driver relationship with associated-with between assessment and driver.                                                                                                |
| node:motivation.principle#1   | judgment_call (0.48)     | missing_attribute (0.71)         | 1.2 | 1.1 | 0.09  | 0.04 | The motivation.principle type has no dedicated attributes for TOGAF-style statement, rationale and implications, so this content is forced into the free-text documentation attribute.                                                                                                         | Add statement, rationale and implications attributes to motivation.principle.                                                                                                                           |

## navigation

| id                                     | validity (p)             | category (p)                     | sev | fix | addr. | clf. | claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | proposed change                                                                                                                                                                 |
