| rel:security.resourceoperation#2          | judgment_call (0.60)     | relationship_direction (0.38) ×2 | 1.2 | 1.4 | 0.18  | 0.07 | The definition says the resource owns its operations, which suggests SecureResource composes ResourceOperation, whereas the catalog currently uses aggregates from secureresource to resourceoperation.                                                                                                                     | Change the secureresource-to-resourceoperation relationship from aggregates to composes.                                                                                      |
| rel:security.dataclassification#1         | judgment_call (0.45)     | relationship_redundant (0.56)    | 1.2 | 1.6 | 0.36  | 0.23 | The governs relationships from security.dataclassification to security.secureresource and security.fieldaccesscontrol overlap with the label-to-asset relationship already held by security.informationentity, since classification applies to resources only indirectly through information entities.                      | Remove the governs relationships to secureresource and fieldaccesscontrol and rely on the informationentity relationship for indirect application to resources.               |
| rel:security.informationright#2           | judgment_call (0.42)     | relationship_direction (0.78)    | 1.2 | 1.4 | 0.31  | 0.33 | The relationship security.informationright --[implements]--> security.permission is questionable because InformationRight is defined as distinct from a role-assigned Permission, so 'implements' does not fit; 'realizes' or 'references' may fit better.                                                                  | Replace the 'implements' predicate with 'realizes' or 'references' for the relationship to security.permission.                                                               |
| node:security.fieldaccesscontrol#4        | judgment_call (0.54)     | relationship_redundant (0.81) ×2 | 1.2 | 1.6 | 0.07  | 0.08 | The governs and protects relationships from security.fieldaccesscontrol to security.secureresource appear redundant with each other.                                                                                                                                                                                        | Keep only one of the governs or protects relationships to secureresource.                                                                                                     |
| node:security.separationofduty#3          | judgment_call (0.61)     | missing_attribute (0.58)         | 1.2 | 1.1 | 0.37  | 0.07 | security.separationofduty lacks a scope attribute, unlike security.bindingofduty which has scope.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Add a scope attribute to security.separationofduty matching bindingofduty.scope.                                                                                              |
| node:security.accountabilityrequirement#4 | judgment_call (0.44)     | definition_conflict (0.53)       | 1.2 | 1.2 | 0.09  | 0.03 | The definition says the type covers under what conditions evidence can be challenged, but the challengeable attribute is only a boolean, so challenge conditions cannot be recorded.                                                                                                                                        | Add a way to record challenge conditions beyond the boolean challengeable.                                                                                                    |
| node:security.fieldaccesscontrol#2        | judgment_call (0.43)     | missing_attribute (0.47)         | 1.1 | 0.9 | 0.11  | 0.07 | security.fieldaccesscontrol has no attribute naming the permission or roles that may see the clear value; this information exists only in the requires relationship.                                                                                                                                                        | Add an attribute naming the permission or roles that can see the clear value.                                                                                                 |
| rel:security.policyrule#4                 | judgment_call (0.62)     | relationship_direction (0.65)    | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.04  | 0.12 | The relationship security.policyrule governs security.informationright is questionable because InformationRight carries its own condition and is arguably independent of PolicyRule.                                                                                                                                        | Reconsider or remove the governs relationship to InformationRight.                                                                                                            |
| rel:security.threat#5                     | judgment_call (0.49)     | relationship_direction (0.59)    | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.16  | 0.05 | The relationships security.threat triggers security.auditconfig and security.threat constrains security.accesscondition describe design motivation rather than runtime triggering or constraint.                                                                                                                            | Replace triggers and constrains with influence.                                                                                                                               |
| node:security.countermeasure#3            | judgment_call (0.47)     | missing_attribute (0.70)         | 1.1 | 1.0 | 0.08  | 0.04 | security.countermeasure lacks a controlId attribute holding a control identifier such as 'SC-8'.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Add a controlId string attribute to security.countermeasure.                                                                                                                  |
| rel:security.evidence#2                   | judgment_call (0.48)     | relationship_direction (0.82)    | 1.1 | 1.3 | 0.04  | 0.09 | The relationship security.evidence --[validates]--> security.policyrule uses a predicate that belongs to the testing category and fits evidence poorly.                                                                                                                                                                     | Replace 'validates' with 'references' or 'supports'.                                                                                                                          |
| rel:security.role#2                       | judgment_call (0.40)     | relationship_redundant (0.69)    | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.10  | 0.05 | The relationship security.role accesses security.secureresource is derivable via Permission and is therefore redundant.                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Remove the accesses relationship from security.role to security.secureresource.                                                                                               |
| rel:security.threat#3                     | judgment_call (0.46)     | relationship_direction (0.83)    | 1.1 | 1.6 | 0.03  | 0.03 | The relationship security.threat maps-to motivation.requirement uses maps-to, a data-schema predicate, for traceability, which misfits its semantics.                                                                                                                                                                       | Replace maps-to with a traceability-appropriate predicate such as influence or associated-with.                                                                               |
| rel:security.accesscondition#3            | judgment_call (0.49)     | relationship_redundant (0.55)    | 1.1 | 0.7 | 0.18  | 0.14 | The relationship security.accesscondition governs security.fieldaccesscontrol partly overlaps in meaning with the constrains relationship.                                                                                                                                                                                  | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.countermeasure#2             | judgment_call (0.62)     | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.10  | 0.03 | The relationship security.countermeasure realizes motivation.goal is too strong for a qualitative goal; supports fits better.                                                                                                                                                                                               | Replace the realizes predicate with supports.                                                                                                                                 |
| node:security.retentionpolicy#4           | judgment_call (0.44)     | type_overlap (0.88)              | 1.1 | 0.6 | 0.28  | 0.34 | security.retentionpolicy overlaps with data-store.retentionpolicy, needing a boundary or precedence rule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.passwordpolicy#2             | judgment_call (0.54)     | relationship_redundant (0.80)    | 1.1 | 1.5 | 0.09  | 0.07 | The mitigates relationship from security.passwordpolicy to security.threat partly duplicates the path PasswordPolicy to Countermeasure to Threat.                                                                                                                                                                           | Remove or reconsider the direct mitigates relationship to Threat in favor of the path via Countermeasure.                                                                     |
| node:security.auditconfig#3               | judgment_call (0.42)     | enum_gap (0.29)                  | 1.1 | 1.7 | 0.25  | 0.06 | The security.auditconfig.level enum value 'none' duplicates the meaning of enabled=false.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Remove the 'none' value from the level enum.                                                                                                                                  |
| rel:security.informationentity#2          | judgment_call (0.52)     | relationship_redundant (0.32)    | 1.1 | 0.6 | 0.16  | 0.13 | The relationship security.informationentity --[constrained-by]--> security.retentionpolicy may stretch the definition of retention policy, since retention policy is scoped to security-relevant data such as audit logs and keys, not general information categories like PII.                                             | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.policyrule#2                 | judgment_call (0.55)     | relationship_redundant (0.65)    | 1.1 | 0.8 | 0.32  | 0.50 | The relationships security.policyrule uses security.accesscondition and security.policyrule uses security.condition are partly redundant because a Condition may itself compose AccessConditions.                                                                                                                           | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.securitypolicy#2             | judgment_call (0.64)     | relationship_direction (0.54)    | 1.0 | 1.5 | 0.10  | 0.05 | The relationship security.securitypolicy aggregates PolicyRule should arguably be composes, since rules do not exist outside their policy.                                                                                                                                                                                  | Change the aggregates predicate to composes.                                                                                                                                  |
| node:security.securityconstraints#1       | judgment_call (0.42)     | missing_attribute (0.57) ×2      | 1.0 | 1.0 | 0.38  | 0.06 | The security.securityconstraints container lacks a scoping attribute (application/scope) to indicate which application it applies to.                                                                                                                                                                                       | Add a scope/application attribute to security.securityconstraints.                                                                                                            |
| rel:security.separationofduty#1           | judgment_call (0.57)     | relationship_redundant (0.71)    | 1.0 | 0.7 | 0.16  | 0.09 | The relationships security.separationofduty constrains business.businessrole and security.separationofduty constrains security.role express the same role-exclusion at different layers, and are likely redundant if security roles map one-to-one to business roles.                                                       | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.dataclassification#2         | judgment_call (0.57)     | relationship_direction (0.59)    | 1.0 | 1.5 | 0.06  | 0.05 | The requires relationship from security.dataclassification to security.countermeasure duplicates the governance notion of 'mandates', which fits better because a classification label does not need a control in order to function.                                                                                        | Replace the 'requires' predicate with 'mandates' for the relationship from dataclassification to countermeasure.                                                              |
| rel:security.secureresource#2             | judgment_call (0.65)     | relationship_direction (0.69) ×2 | 1.0 | 1.4 | 0.06  | 0.04 | The aggregates relationship from security.secureresource to security.resourceoperation is semantically weak because a ResourceOperation is declared on a single resource and cannot exist without it, so composes fits the lifecycle dependency better.                                                                     | Change the predicate of secureresource -> resourceoperation from aggregates to composes.                                                                                      |
| node:security.policyaction#4              | judgment_call (0.50)     | type_overlap (0.48)              | 1.0 | 0.7 | 0.28  | 0.29 | The 'rate-limit' value of security.policyaction.type overlaps with api.ratelimit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.secureresource#1             | judgment_call (0.63)     | relationship_direction (0.70)    | 1.0 | 1.4 | 0.07  | 0.04 | The aggregates relationship from security.secureresource to security.fieldaccesscontrol is semantically weak because a FieldAccessControl is bound to one field of a single resource and cannot exist without it, so composes fits the lifecycle dependency better.                                                         | Change the predicate of secureresource -> fieldaccesscontrol from aggregates to composes.                                                                                     |
| rel:security.securitymodel#4              | judgment_call (0.57)     | relationship_direction (0.80)    | 1.0 | 1.4 | 0.14  | 0.04 | The constrains relationship from SecurityModel to AccountabilityRequirement has weak semantics because a container does not restrict its members; aggregates would fit better.                                                                                                                                              | Replace constrains with aggregates for SecurityModel to AccountabilityRequirement.                                                                                            |
| rel:security.bindingofduty#1              | judgment_call (0.58)     | relationship_redundant (0.59)    | 1.0 | 1.7 | 0.07  | 0.08 | The constrains relationship (bindingofduty to role) and the governs relationship (bindingofduty to resourceoperation) both express the rule restricting access through the influence family, differing only in target, and could be unified.                                                                                | Unify the constrains and governs relationships into a single relationship type.                                                                                               |
| node:security.policyaction#3              | judgment_call (0.42)     | type_overlap (0.62)              | 1.0 | 0.5 | 0.58  | 0.69 | The 'mask' value of security.policyaction.type overlaps with the FieldAccessControl type.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.condition#1                  | judgment_call (0.63)     | relationship_direction (0.77)    | 1.0 | 1.2 | 0.23  | 0.29 | The 'composes' relationship from security.condition to security.accesscondition may be too strong, because AccessConditions are also evaluated on their own within PolicyRules, ResourceOperations and InformationRights and so exist independently of a Condition; 'aggregates' would fit better.                          | Change the predicate of security.condition -> security.accesscondition from 'composes' to 'aggregates'.                                                                       |
| node:security.accesscondition#2           | judgment_call (0.38)     | attribute_naming (0.85)          | 1.0 | 1.7 | 0.10  | 0.09 | The attribute security.accesscondition.source has a name that collides with the sibling 'source' source-code-reference concept while meaning something different.                                                                                                                                                           | Rename source to something like attributeSource.                                                                                                                              |
| rel:security.needtoknow#2                 | judgment_call (0.53)     | relationship_direction (0.84)    | 1.0 | 1.3 | 0.09  | 0.05 | The relationship security.needtoknow associated-with security.role uses a vague predicate; a more specific predicate such as constrains would better express that need-to-know narrows role-based access.                                                                                                                   | Replace the associated-with predicate with a more specific one such as constrains.                                                                                            |
| rel:security.bindingofduty#3              | judgment_call (0.66)     | relationship_direction (0.57)    | 0.9 | 1.3 | 0.21  | 0.14 | Binding of duty and separation of duty are complementary and should be modelled as such rather than merely 'associated-with'.                                                                                                                                                                                               | Replace the generic associated-with predicate between them with a predicate expressing complementarity.                                                                       |
| node:security.threat#3                    | judgment_call (0.40)     | attribute_naming (0.87)          | 0.9 | 1.7 | 0.27  | 0.08 | The security.threat attribute 'source' (a code reference) collides in name with the NIST SP 800-30 concept 'threat source' (the adversary).                                                                                                                                                                                 | Rename 'source' to something like 'code_reference' or 'source_ref' to avoid collision with threat source.                                                                     |
| rel:security.resourceoperation#4          | judgment_call (0.49)     | relationship_direction (0.82)    | 0.9 | 1.1 | 0.10  | 0.07 | The triggers relationship from security.resourceoperation to security.auditconfig is semantically loose, since an operation triggers audit events rather than the configuration.                                                                                                                                            | Replace the triggers predicate with uses or references.                                                                                                                       |

| node:security.auditconfig#5               | judgment_call (0.47)     | attribute_naming (0.83)          | 0.9 | 1.3 | 0.14  | 0.25 | The source object attribute on security.auditconfig has a different meaning from the source string attribute on Evidence, causing a naming collision.                                                                                                                                                                       | Rename or harmonize security.auditconfig.source to disambiguate it from Evidence's source string.                                                                             |
| node:security.evidence#3                  | judgment_call (0.52)     | attribute_naming (0.79)          | 0.9 | 1.6 | 0.09  | 0.10 | The required string attribute 'source' on security.evidence conflicts with sibling 'source' attributes elsewhere that are source-code reference objects, so it should be renamed to 'origin'.                                                                                                                               | Rename the attribute 'source' to 'origin'.                                                                                                                                    |
| node:security.securitymodel#5             | judgment_call (0.51)     | missing_attribute (0.56)         | 0.9 | 1.0 | 0.06  | 0.04 | security.securitymodel lacks a source attribute although most sibling types have one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Add a source attribute to securitymodel consistent with siblings.                                                                                                             |
| rel:security.accountabilityrequirement#2  | judgment_call (0.49)     | relationship_direction (0.78)    | 0.9 | 0.5 | 0.17  | 0.09 | The 'constrains' relationship from security.accountabilityrequirement to security.actor is loosely used, because the obligation restricts how the actor may act rather than limiting the actor itself.                                                                                                                      | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |

| rel:security.bindingofduty#2              | judgment_call (0.50)     | relationship_direction (0.74)    | 0.8 | 0.8 | 0.07  | 0.06 | The requires relationship from bindingofduty to evidence is labelled ArchiMate Access, but for Evidence it is better read as a data dependency.                                                                                                                                                                             | Relabel the requires predicate mapping from ArchiMate Access to a data dependency reading.                                                                                    |
| rel:security.auditconfig#3                | judgment_call (0.57)     | relationship_direction (0.69)    | 0.8 | 1.2 | 0.23  | 0.17 | The predicate triggers from security.auditconfig to security.evidence would read better as generates, since the config emits the artifact.                                                                                                                                                                                  | Replace the triggers predicate with generates for auditconfig to evidence.                                                                                                    |
| rel:security.accountabilityrequirement#1  | judgment_call (0.46)     | relationship_redundant (0.40)    | 0.8 | 0.4 | 0.22  | 0.24 | The three 'requires' relationships from security.accountabilityrequirement to AuditConfig, Evidence and RetentionPolicy partly overlap, because Evidence such as audit trails is produced by AuditConfig and kept under RetentionPolicy.                                                                                    | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.securitypolicy#4             | judgment_call (0.51)     | relationship_redundant (0.54)    | 0.8 | 0.5 | 0.06  | 0.05 | The relationships security.securitypolicy realizes Principle and security.securitypolicy satisfies Requirement may overlap.                                                                                                                                                                                                 | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| rel:security.securityconstraints#4        | judgment_call (0.61)     | relationship_direction (0.68)    | 0.8 | 1.2 | 0.06  | 0.04 | The implements relationship from security.securityconstraints to motivation.constraint would read more naturally as realizes.                                                                                                                                                                                               | Replace implements with realizes.                                                                                                                                             |
| node:security.passwordpolicy#3            | judgment_call (0.35)     | definition_conflict (0.50)       | 0.6 | 0.1 | 0.44  | 0.05 | Lockout (lockoutAttempts, lockoutDuration) in security.passwordpolicy belongs to NIST control AC-7, not IA-5(1), which the type definition cites.                                                                                                                                                                           | Correct the definition and standard reference so lockout is attributed to AC-7 rather than IA-5(1).                                                                           |

| node:security.fieldaccesscontrol#1        | judgment_call (0.40)     | definition_conflict (0.29)       | 0.5 | 0.1 | 0.28  | 0.03 | The maskingStrategy attribute of security.fieldaccesscontrol cites NIST SC-28 (protection at rest), but masking on exposure is closer to AC-3/AC-21.                                                                                                                                                                        | Re-cite maskingStrategy to NIST AC-3/AC-21 instead of SC-28.                                                                                                                  |
| node:security.resourceoperation#1         | works_as_designed (0.43) | relationship_redundant (0.68)    | 1.7 | 1.9 | 0.08  | 0.09 | The outgoing 'accesses' and 'governs' relationships from security.resourceoperation to security.secureresource, plus the incoming 'aggregates' relationship, express the same meaning and are redundant.                                                                                                                    | Keep only one of the accesses, governs and aggregates relationships and remove the others.                                                                                    |

| node:security.accountabilityrequirement#1 | works_as_designed (0.45) | missing_attribute (0.37)         | 1.2 | 0.7 | 0.33  | 0.07 | security.accountabilityrequirement has no attribute identifying the obligated actor even though its definition says 'who'; it depends on the constrains→actor relationship.                                                                                                                                                 | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| node:security.threat#4                    | works_as_designed (0.49) | missing_attribute (0.46) ×2      | 1.2 | 1.0 | 0.23  | 0.07 | security.threat lacks an attribute for the threat source (the adversary) as defined in NIST SP 800-30.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Add a threat source attribute (adversary type) to security.threat.                                                                                                            |
| node:security.validationrule#4            | works_as_designed (0.34) | type_overlap (0.80)              | 1.1 | 0.4 | 0.74  | 0.48 | The boundary between security.validationrule and data-model schema constraints is undefined and needs a rule.                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Add a rule that only security-motivated validation is modeled in security.validationrule, leaving other constraints to the data-model layer.                                  |
| node:security.secureresource#1            | works_as_designed (0.38) | type_overlap (0.75)              | 1.0 | 0.3 | 0.82  | 0.66 | security.secureresource overlaps with security.informationentity, and the spec should state that SecureResource is the system-level container while InformationEntity is the logical asset.                                                                                                                                 | Add a boundary statement to the definitions: SecureResource is the system-level container, InformationEntity is the logical information asset.                                |
| node:security.passwordpolicy#4            | works_as_designed (0.37) | missing_attribute (0.51)         | 1.0 | 0.9 | 0.49  | 0.22 | security.passwordpolicy lacks an attribute for breached-password screening.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Consider adding an attribute for breached-password screening.                                                                                                                 |
| node:security.accountabilityrequirement#3 | works_as_designed (0.50) | attribute_naming (0.38)          | 0.9 | 0.4 | 0.57  | 0.32 | The attribute purposeDeclaration on security.accountabilityrequirement overlaps with NeedToKnow.objective.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | none stated                                                                                                                                                                   |
| node:security.needtoknow#3                | works_as_designed (0.43) | attribute_naming (0.85)          | 0.8 | 1.4 | 0.20  | 0.12 | The attribute name 'objective' on security.needtoknow collides with Actor.objectives.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Rename 'objective' to 'purpose'.                                                                                                                                              |
| node:security.actor#6                     | works_as_designed (0.43) | definition_conflict (0.22)       | 0.8 | 0.1 | 0.78  | 0.19 | Modellers may confuse security.actor with individual end users, since the definition does not make clear that actors can represent populations, services or external parties.                                                                                                                                               | Clarify the definition to say that an actor is a principal or population, not just an individual end user.                                                                    |

## technology

| id                                        | validity (p)             | category (p)                     | sev | fix | addr. | clf. | claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | proposed change                                                                                                                                                                               |

| node:technology.technologyinterface#1     | real_defect (0.35)       | enum_gap (0.89)                  | 2.1 | 1.0 | 0.04  | 0.04 | The protocol enum on technology.technologyinterface lacks common infrastructure wire protocols such as kafka, redis, postgres/sql and ssh.                                                                                                                                       | Add kafka, redis, postgres/sql and ssh values to the protocol enum.                                                                                                                           |
| node:technology.communicationnetwork#2    | real_defect (0.58)       | enum_gap (0.88) ×2               | 2.0 | 1.0 | 0.33  | 0.05 | The networkType enum of technology.communicationnetwork has no value that fits cloud VPCs, subnets or container overlay networks.                                                                                                                                                | Add 'virtual' and/or 'overlay' values to networkType.                                                                                                                                         |
| node:technology.systemsoftware#1          | real_defect (0.42)       | enum_gap (0.87) ×2               | 2.0 | 1.0 | 0.25  | 0.08 | The softwareType enum of technology.systemsoftware lacks values for language runtimes (JVM, Node.js, CPython), web servers/reverse proxies (nginx) and application servers, even though the layer description lists frameworks and runtimes.                                     | Add enum values such as language-runtime, web-server and application-server to softwareType.                                                                                                  |
| node:technology.path#3                    | real_defect (0.60)       | relationship_direction (0.56)    | 2.0 | 1.7 | 0.08  | 0.11 | The relationships 'realizes technologyservice', 'flows-to technologyservice' and 'assigned-to technologyinterface' from technology.path are not ArchiMate-valid for a structure element.                                                                                         | Review these relationships and remove or replace those that are not ArchiMate-valid.                                                                                                          |
| node:technology.technologycollaboration#3 | real_defect (0.59)       | relationship_direction (0.54) ×2 | 1.9 | 1.6 | 0.18  | 0.11 | The relationship 'communicationnetwork assigned-to technologycollaboration' is not valid ArchiMate.                                                                                                                                                                              | Remove or replace this relationship with one that is valid in ArchiMate.                                                                                                                      |
| node:technology.artifact#3                | real_defect (0.56)       | enum_gap (0.53) ×3               | 1.8 | 1.0 | 0.23  | 0.07 | The definition of technology.artifact names 'executable/binary' but the artifactType enum has no such value.                                                                                                                                                                     | Add an 'executable' (or 'binary') value to artifactType.                                                                                                                                      |
| rel:technology.technologyinterface#1      | real_defect (0.64)       | relationship_direction (0.56)    | 1.8 | 1.8 | 0.17  | 0.04 | The relationships technologyinterface assigned-to node and technologyinterface assigned-to systemsoftware invert the ArchiMate convention, in which nodes and system software are composed of or assigned their interfaces.                                                      | Replace the interface-to-node and interface-to-systemsoftware assigned-to relationships with composed-of (node/systemsoftware composes interface), or reverse their direction.                |
| node:technology.technologycollaboration#2 | real_defect (0.46)       | relationship_direction (0.77) ×3 | 1.7 | 1.6 | 0.18  | 0.08 | The relationship 'technologyinteraction realizes technologycollaboration' inverts ArchiMate, where a collaboration is assigned to an interaction.                                                                                                                                | Replace it with technologycollaboration assigned-to technologyinteraction.                                                                                                                    |
| node:technology.communicationnetwork#1    | real_defect (0.67)       | enum_gap (0.81) ×2               | 1.7 | 1.9 | 0.07  | 0.04 | The networkType enum of technology.communicationnetwork mixes orthogonal axes: scope (lan/wan), technology (vpn/wireless) and ownership (internet/intranet).                                                                                                                     | Split networkType into separate scope and medium (and ownership) attributes, or otherwise separate the axes.                                                                                  |
| rel:technology.technologyinteraction#2    | real_defect (0.48)       | relationship_direction (0.52) ×2 | 1.7 | 1.5 | 0.28  | 0.17 | The relationship technologyinteraction composes technologyfunction is questionable because a function is performed by a single node, so a multi-participant interaction being part of a function conflicts with both definitions.                                                | Replace composes with aggregation or flow-to between technologyinteraction and technologyfunction.                                                                                            |
| rel:technology.technologyinteraction#1    | real_defect (0.76)       | relationship_direction (0.60) ×3 | 1.6 | 1.8 | 0.18  | 0.05 | The relationship technologyinteraction realizes technologycollaboration is semantically wrong because behavior does not realize active structure; in ArchiMate the collaboration is assigned to the interaction.                                                                 | Remove the realizes relationship to technologycollaboration and rely on the collaboration-to-interaction assignment (the existing performs relationship, as the inverse of assigned-to).      |

| node:technology.technologycollaboration#5 | judgment_call (0.48)     | missing_attribute (0.65)         | 0.7 | 0.9 | 0.13  | 0.04 | TechnologyCollaboration lacks a memberCount attribute to capture how many members participate.                                                                                                                                                                                   | Add a memberCount attribute.                                                                                                                                                                  |

| rel:testing.targetinputfield#1         | real_defect (0.54)       | relationship_direction (0.78)    | 1.6 | 1.7 | 0.20  | 0.06 | The maps-to relationship from testing.targetinputfield to ux.actioncomponent is wrong because input fields correspond to UI input components rather than action controls, and maps-to is a data schema predicate.                                                                                      | Remove or retarget the maps-to relationship to a UI input component type and use a predicate that fits the semantics.                                                                        |

| node:ux.statetransition#2     | real_defect (0.42)       | enum_gap (0.86) ×2               | 2.0 | 1.0 | 0.03  | 0.05 | The ux.statetransition trigger enum lacks timeout and data events (API success or failure), and 'load' is only a weak proxy for them.                                                                                                                                                  | Add timeout and data-event values (e.g. api-success, api-failure) to the trigger enum.                                                                                                  |
